=== PROMPT ===
You will be given a competitive programming problem.
For a positive rational number x, define f(x) as follows:

Express x as \dfrac{P}{Q} using coprime positive integers P and Q. f(x) is defined as the value P\times Q.

You are given a positive integer N and a sequence A=(A_1,A_2,\dots,A_{N-1}) of positive integers of length N-1.
We call a sequence S=(S_1,S_2,\dots,S_N) of positive integers of length N a good sequence if it satisfies all of the following conditions:

- For every integer i with 1\leq i\leq N-1, it holds that f\left(\dfrac{S_i}{S_{i+1}}\right)=A_i.
- \gcd(S_1,S_2,\dots,S_N)=1.

Define the score of a sequence as the product of all its elements.
It can be proved that there are finitely many good sequences. Find the sum, modulo 998244353, of the scores of all good sequences.

Input

The input is given from Standard Input in the following format:
N
A_1 A_2 \dots A_{N-1}

Output

Print the sum, modulo 998244353, of the scores of all good sequences.

Constraints


- 2\leq N\leq 1000
- 1\leq A_i\leq 1000 (1\leq i\leq N-1)
- All input values are integers.

Sample Input 1

6
1 9 2 2 9

Sample Output 1

939634344

For example, both (2,2,18,9,18,2) and (18,18,2,1,2,18) are good sequences, and both have a score of 23328.
There are a total of 16 good sequences, and the sum of the scores of all of them is 939634344.

Sample Input 2

2
9

Sample Output 2

18

There are 2 good sequences, both with a score of 9.

Sample Input 3

25
222 299 229 22 999 922 99 992 22 292 222 229 992 922 22 992 222 222 99 29 92 999 2 29

Sample Output 3

192457116

Do not forget to compute the sum modulo 998244353.

Reason in a commit-implement-recheck way.

A productive rhythm for this task usually looks like this:
- Isolate the decisive constraints, the target complexity, and at most one or two approaches that are still truly plausible.
- If two candidates remain, compare them only on the decisive constraint and commit quickly to the stronger one instead of keeping a wide search open.
- Before coding, keep only a tiny implementation ledger for the chosen plan: main invariant, key state or transition, and the one or two edge cases most likely to fail.
- Move into code as soon as that ledger is clear. After coding starts, keep reasoning attached to the current draft rather than reopening the high-level search.
- After the first complete draft, do one short implementation-check-adjust-check loop on the same draft: test the key edge case, patch locally if needed, and recheck.
- Reopen the main design only if a concrete counterexample breaks the core invariant. Otherwise prefer the local patch and keep closing.
- Once the current draft, invariant, and short recheck still agree, finalize the solution.

Output only the final Python code enclosed in a code block.

prime_exps = [dict() for _ in range(N-1)]
total_exp = defaultdict(int)
for idx,a in enumerate(A):

   while x>1:

Need compute F. Let's design DP carefully for speed.

Function calc(p, b, B):
    qpow = [1]*(B+1)
    for v in range(1,B+1): qpow[v] = qpow[v-1]*p % MOD
    dp0 = [0]*(B+1)
    dp1 = [0]*(B+1)
    # initialize position 1
    for v in range(1,B+1):
        dp0[v] = qpow[v]
    dp1[0] = 1  # qpow[0]=1
    # But wait if B=0, dp1[0]=1, dp0 empty.
    for bi in b:
        ndp0 = [0]*(B+1)
        ndp1 = [0]*(B+1)
        if bi == 0:
            # u=v
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0=dp0[v]; w1=dp1[v]
                if w0==0 and w1==0: continue
                if v == 0:
                    # only dp1[0] can be nonzero; dp0[0]=0 always
                    ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0 + w1) % MOD  # qpow[0]=1
                else:
                    qv = qpow[v]
                    if w0:
                        ndp0[v] = (ndp0[v] + w0*qv) % MOD
                    if w1:
                        ndp1[v] = (ndp1[v] + w1*qv) % MOD
        else:
            # up and down
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0=dp0[v]; w1=dp1[v]
                if not (w0 or w1): continue
                # up u=v+bi
                u = v + bi
                if u <= B:
                    qv = qpow[u]
                    if w0:
                        ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0*qv) % MOD
                    if w1:
                        ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1*qv) % MOD
                # down u=v-bi
                u = v - bi
                if u >= 0:
                    qv = qpow[u]  # qpow[0]=1
                    if u == 0:
                        # zero seen now
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0 + w1) % MOD
                    else:
                        if w0:
                            ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0*qv) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1*qv) % MOD
        dp0, dp1 = ndp0, ndp1
    return sum(dp1) % MOD

Need ensure dp0[0] always 0. Initialize dp0[0]=0. Transitions to u=0 go to ndp1, not ndp0. For b=0 and v=0, if w0=0, w1=1, ndp1[0]+=1. Good. For b>0 down to u=0, add w0+w1 to ndp1[0]. Good.

But in bi==0 branch, if v==0, w0 should be 0, but if due to some bug dp0[0] maybe nonzero? We never set dp0[0]. So okay. But if w0 nonzero and v==0? no. We can simplify.

Potential issue: For b=0, from v>0, if previous flag false, new value same v>0, no zero seen, so ndp0[v] += w0*q^v. If previous flag true, ndp1[v]+=w1*q^v. Correct. If v=0, previous flag must be true; new zero seen remains true; ndp1[0]+=w1*q^0. Good.

But wait: For b=0, if previous flag false and v=0 impossible; if previous flag true and v=0, okay.

Now, is the weight q^u applied for every vertex including s_1? Yes init dp0[v]=q^v, dp1[0]=1. Then each transition multiplies q^{s_{i+1}}. So after processing all edges, dp includes q^{sum s_i}. Good.

Potential issue: In DP, for each edge, we multiply by qpow[u] for the new vertex. But if b=0 and v>0, qv=qpow[v]. If previous flag false and v>0, ndp0[v]+=w0*qv. But what if v=0 and previous flag false? dp0[0]=0. Good.

Let's write a more efficient calc function:

def calc(p, b, B):
    qpow = [1]*(B+1)
    for i in range(1,B+1):
        qpow[i] = qpow[i-1] * p % MOD

    dp0 = [0]*(B+1)
    dp1 = [0]*(B+1)
    for v in range(1, B+1):
        dp0[v] = qpow[v]
    dp1[0] = 1

    for bi in b:
        ndp0 = [0]*(B+1)
        ndp1 = [0]*(B+1)
        if bi == 0:
            # only same value
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                if w0:
                    if v:
                        ndp0[v] = (ndp0[v] + w0 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        # v=0, dp0[0] should be 0, but handle
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if w1:
                    if v:
                        ndp1[v] = (ndp1[v] + w1 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
        else:
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if not (w0 or w1):
                    continue
                u = v + bi
                if u <= B:
                    qv = qpow[u]
                    if w0:
                        ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                    if w1:
                        ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
                u = v - bi
                if u >= 0:
                    if u == 0:
                        if w0:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
                    else:
                        qv = qpow[u]
                        if w0:
                            ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
        dp0, dp1 = ndp0, ndp1
    return sum(dp1) % MOD

But note in bi==0 branch, if v=0, dp1[0] nonzero; we add to ndp1[0] with qpow[0]=1. If dp0[0] somehow nonzero, also add to ndp1[0] because zero seen. Good.

Could combine bi==0 with generic up/down? If we treat b=0, up and down both u=v; would double count. So separate.

Now, let's consider if B is 0 and b list all zeros. calc not called because total B=0 skip. If called, B=0, qpow=[1], dp0=[0], dp1=[1]; for bi=0, ndp1[0]=1; return 1. Fine.

Now, factorization. Let's implement SPF:

MAXA = 1000
spf = list(range(MAXA+1))
for i in range(2, int(MAXA**0.5)+1):
    if spf[i] == i:
        for j in range(i*i, MAXA+1, i):
            if spf[j] == j:
                spf[j] = i

Then factor:
prime_exps = []
total_exp = {}
for a in A:
    x = a
    d = {}
    while x > 1:
        p = spf[x]
        cnt = 0
        while x % p == 0:
            x //= p
            cnt += 1
        d[p] = cnt
        total_exp[p] = total_exp.get(p, 0) + cnt
    prime_exps.append(d)

Since A_i positive, x eventually 1. For a=1, d empty.

Then ans=1
for p, B in total_exp.items():
    b = [prime_exps[i].get(p, 0) for i in range(N-1)]
    F = calc(p, b, B)
    ans = ans * F % MOD
print(ans)

Now, let's think about optimizing DP further by using arrays from `list` and local variable references. We can write:

def calc(p, b, B, MOD=998244353):
    qpow = [1]*(B+1)
    for i in range(1,B+1):
        qpow[i] = qpow[i-1] * p % MOD
    dp0 = [0]*(B+1)
    dp1 = [0]*(B+1)
    for v in range(1,B+1):
        dp0[v] = qpow[v]
    dp1[0] = 1
    for bi in b:
        ndp0 = [0]*(B+1)
        ndp1 = [0]*(B+1)
        if bi == 0:
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                if w0:
                    if v:
                        ndp0[v] = (ndp0[v] + w0 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if w1:
                    if v:
                        ndp1[v] = (ndp1[v] + w1 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
        else:
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if not (w0 or w1):
                    continue
                u = v + bi
                if u <= B:
                    qv = qpow[u]
                    if w0:
                        ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                    if w1:
                        ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
                u = v - bi
                if u >= 0:
                    if u == 0:
                        if w0:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
                    else:
                        qv = qpow[u]
                        if w0:
                            ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
        dp0, dp1 = ndp0, ndp1
    return sum(dp1) % MOD

Code structure:

import sys
MOD = 998244353

def build_spf(limit=1000):
    spf = list(range(limit+1))
    if limit >= 1:
        spf[1]=1
    for i in range(2, int(limit**0.5)+1):
        if spf[i] == i:
            step = i
            start = i*i
            for j in range(start, limit+1, step):
                if spf[j] == j:
                    spf[j] = i
    return spf

def calc(p, b, B):
    qpow = [1]*(B+1)
    for i in range(1, B+1):
        qpow[i] = (qpow[i-1] * p) % MOD
    dp0 = [0]*(B+1)
    dp1 = [0]*(B+1)
    for v in range(1, B+1):
        dp0[v] = qpow[v]
    dp1[0] = 1
    for bi in b:
        ndp0 = [0]*(B+1)
        ndp1 = [0]*(B+1)
        if bi == 0:
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                if w0:
                    if v:
                        ndp0[v] = (ndp0[v] + w0 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if w1:
                    if v:
                        ndp1[v] = (ndp1[v] + w1 * qpow[v]) % MOD
                    else:
                        ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
        else:
            for v in range(B+1):
                w0 = dp0[v]
                w1 = dp1[v]
                if not (w0 or w1):
                    continue
                u = v + bi
                if u <= B:
                    qv = qpow[u]
                    if w0:
                        ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                    if w1:
                        ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
                u = v - bi
                if u >= 0:
                    if u == 0:
                        if w0:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w0) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[0] = (ndp1[0] + w1) % MOD
                    else:
                        qv = qpow[u]
                        if w0:
                            ndp0[u] = (ndp0[u] + w0 * qv) % MOD
                        if w1:
                            ndp1[u] = (ndp1[u] + w1 * qv) % MOD
        dp0, dp1 = ndp0, ndp1
    return sum(dp1) % MOD

def main():
    data = list(map(int, sys.stdin.read().split()))
    if not data: return
    N = data[0]
    A = data[1:]
    spf = build_spf()
    prime_exps = []
    total_exp = {}
    for a in A:
        x = a
        d = {}
        while x > 1:
            p = spf[x]
            cnt = 0
            while x % p == 0:
                x //= p
                cnt += 1
            d[p] = cnt
            total_exp[p] = total_exp.get(p, 0) + cnt
        prime_exps.append(d)
    ans = 1
    for p, B in total_exp.items():
        b = [prime_exps[i].get(p, 0) for i in range(N-1)]
        F = calc(p, b, B)
        ans = ans * F % MOD
    print(ans)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
